## Output-Based Pricing System Regulations {#output-based-pricing-system-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2019-266
:::

[ENVIRONMENTAL VIOLATIONS ADMINISTRATIVE MONETARY PENALTIES
ACT](/eng/acts/E-12.5)

[GREENHOUSE GAS POLLUTION PRICING ACT](/eng/acts/G-11.55)

Registration 2019-06-28

Output-Based Pricing System Regulations

P.C. 2019-974 2019-06-27
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of the Environment, makes the annexed Output-Based Pricing
System Regulations pursuant to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} sections 192, 193, 198 and 256 of the [Greenhouse Gas
  Pollution Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-3538_hq_24019){#fn_81000-2-3538_hq_24019-ID0EBABDA};
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} section 5[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-3538-0507_hq_24952){#fn_81000-2-3538-0507_hq_24952-ID0EEAADA}
  of the [Environmental Violations Administrative Monetary Penalties
  Act](/eng/acts/E-12.5)[[Footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81000-2-3538-E_hq_24617){#fn_81000-2-3538-E_hq_24617-ID0EBAADA}.

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1183989 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (6), for the purposes of the Act and these Regulations,
  [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} all of the following elements that are operated in
    an integrated way to carry out a specified industrial activity:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a site, or multiple sites, at which the specified
      industrial activity is carried out and the buildings, equipment
      and other structures and stationary items located on the site or
      sites, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other sites used in conjunction with the
      specified industrial activity, including a quarry, tailings pond,
      wastewater lagoon or pond and landfill; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the portion of a natural gas transmission pipeline
    system within a province, used to transmit processed natural gas, of
    which the pipelines and associated installations or equipment ---
    including compressor stations, storage installations and compressors
    --- are operated in an integrated way, but excludes pipelines,
    installations or equipment that are used in the local distribution
    of natural gas and that are downstream of a metering station.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}More than one person responsible ---
  paragraph 1(a)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If more than one person is responsible for the
  elements referred to in subparagraph (1)(a)(i) or (ii) as an owner or
  otherwise, including having the charge, management or control of, or
  as the true decision maker with respect to their operations, those
  elements are only included in the definition of facility if there is
  at least one person who is responsible for, owns, has the charge
  management or control of, or is the true decision maker in common.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}More than one person responsible ---
  paragraph 1(b)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If more than one person is responsible for the
  pipelines and associated installations or equipment referred to in
  paragraph (1)(b) as an owner or otherwise, including having the
  charge, management or control of, or as the true decision maker with
  respect to the pipelines and associated installations or equipment,
  those pipelines and associated installations or equipment are only
  included in the definition of facility if there is at least one person
  who is responsible for, owns, has the charge management or control of,
  or is the true decision maker in common.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single facility

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If two or more facilities referred to in
  paragraph (b) of the definition [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in
  subsection (1) within the same province have the same person
  responsible, or, if they have more than one person responsible, they
  have at least one person responsible in common, and are operated in an
  integrated way, they are deemed to be a single facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), with respect to a facility

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any part of a public road or of a railway track
    that is bordered on both sides by the facility and used to carry out
    the facility's specified industrial activities is deemed to be part
    of the facility;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, any part of a railway track
    that is used exclusively to carry out the facility's specified
    industrial activities is part of the facility;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for greater certainty, buildings that are used for
    legal, administrative or management purposes and that are not
    located where a specified industrial activity is carried out are not
    included for the purposes of the definition
    [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection (1); and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if two or more facilities referred to in
    paragraph (b) of the definition [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    in subsection (1), within the same province, have the same person
    responsible or a person responsible in common and are not operated
    in an integrated way, they each constitute a separate facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special case

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a facility that is not a covered
  facility, the specified industrial activities referred to in
  subsections (1) and (5) are those that would be specified industrial
  activities if the facility was a covered facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Greenhouse Gas Pollution
      Pricing Act](/eng/acts/G-11.55). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[additional industrial activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[additional industrial activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      industrial activity that is not set out in column 1 of Schedule 1,
      that is recognized by the Minister, including for the purposes of
      a facility's designation as a covered facility under subsection
      172(1) of the Act, and that is engaged in in a sector that is
      recognized by the Minister as being at significant risk of
      competitiveness impacts resulting from carbon pricing and of
      carbon leakage resulting from carbon pricing. ([activité
      industrielle additionnelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ANSI National Accreditation Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ANSI National Accreditation Board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the accreditation organization that is a subsidiary of the
      American National Standards Institute. ([ANSI National
      Accreditation Board]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[authorized official]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorized official]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person responsible for a
        covered facility who is an individual, that individual or
        another individual who is authorized to act on their behalf;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person responsible for a
        covered facility that is a corporation, an officer of the
        corporation who is authorized to act on its behalf; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a person responsible for a
        covered facility that is another entity, an individual who is
        authorized to act on its behalf. ([agent
        autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[biomass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[biomass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means plants or plant materials,
      animal waste or any product made of either of these, including
      wood and wood products, bio-charcoal, agricultural residues,
      biologically derived organic matter in municipal and industrial
      wastes, landfill gas, bio-alcohols, pulping liquor, sludge
      digestion gas and fuel from animal or plant
      origin. ([biomasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[boiler unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[boiler unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      subsection 2(1) of the Regulations Limiting Carbon Dioxide
      Emissions from Natural Gas-fired Generation of
      Electricity. ([groupe chaudière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[combustion engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[combustion engine]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an engine, other
      than an engine that is self-propelled or designed to be propelled
      while performing its function, that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} operates according to the Brayton thermodynamic
        cycle and combusts fossil fuels to produce a net amount of
        motive power; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} combusts fossil fuels and uses reciprocating
        motion to convert thermal energy into mechanical work. ([moteur
        à combustion]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[combustion engine unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[combustion engine unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as subsection 2(1) of the Regulations Limiting Carbon
      Dioxide Emissions from Natural Gas-fired Generation of
      Electricity. ([groupe moteur à combustion]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Directive 017]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Directive 017]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2023-240, s.
      2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Directive PNG017]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Directive PNG017]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2023-240, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[distribution system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distribution system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning
      as section 3 of the Act. ([réseau de
      distribution]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dolomitic lime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dolomitic lime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means lime derived from
      limestone that contains equal to or more than 5% magnesium
      carbonate. ([chaux dolomitique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[electricity generation facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[electricity generation facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      covered facility, other than one whose primary activity is
      something other than an industrial activity, that generates
      electricity as its primary industrial activity, used to generate
      electricity from fossil fuels and composed of one unit or a group
      of units. ([installation de production
      d'électricité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[flaring emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[flaring emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means controlled
      emissions of gases from industrial activities as a result of the
      combustion of a gas or liquid stream produced at a facility, the
      purpose of which is not to produce useful heat. It does not
      include emissions from the flaring of landfill gas. ([émissions de
      torchage]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gaseous fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gaseous fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fossil fuel that is
      gaseous at a temperature of 15°C and a pressure of
      101.325kPa. ([combustible gazeux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GHG]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GHG]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means greenhouse gas. ([version
      anglaise seulement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[GHGRP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GHGRP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2023-240, s.
      2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[global warming potential]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[global warming potential]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[GWP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the global warming potential set
      out in column 2 of Schedule 3 to the Act for the greenhouse gas
      set out in column 1 of that Schedule. ([PRP]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"} ou [potentiel de réchauffement
      planétaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[grey cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[grey cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mix composed primarily
      of clinker that contains more than 0.5% by weight of ferric oxide,
      gypsum and limestone. ([ciment gris]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[HFC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[HFC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the hydrofluorocarbons set out
      in items 6 to 24 of Schedule 3 to the Act. ([HFC]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[high-calcium lime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[high-calcium lime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means lime derived from
      limestone that contains less than 5% magnesium carbonate. ([chaux
      forte en calcium]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[industrial process emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[industrial process emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      emissions from an industrial process that involves a chemical or
      physical reaction other than combustion and the purpose of which
      is not to produce useful heat. ([émissions liées aux procédés
      industriels]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[industrial product use emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[industrial product use emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      emissions from the use of a product in an industrial process that
      does not involve a chemical or physical reaction and does not
      react in the process, including emissions from the use of sulphur
      hexafluoride (SF~6~), HFCs and PFCs as cover gases and the use of
      HFCs and PFCs in a foam-blowing process. ([émissions associées à
      l'utilisation de produits industriels]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[IPCC Guidelines]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[IPCC Guidelines]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2023-240, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[ISO Standard 14065]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO Standard 14065]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the standard ISO
      14065 entitled Greenhouse gases --- Requirements for greenhouse
      gas validation and verification bodies for use in accreditation or
      other forms of recognition, published by the International
      Organization for Standardization in 2013. ([norme ISO
      14065]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[leakage emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[leakage emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means uncontrolled
      emissions. It does not include industrial process emissions and
      industrial product use emissions. ([émissions dues aux
      fuites]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[liquid fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liquid fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fossil fuel that is
      liquid at a temperature of 15°C and a pressure of
      101.325kPa. ([combustible liquide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture of
      hydrocarbons --- such as methane, ethane or propane --- that is in
      a gaseous state at a temperature of 15°C and a pressure of 101.325
      kPa and that is composed of at least 70% methane by volume or that
      has a higher heating value that is not less than 35 MJ/standard
      m^3^ and not more than 41 MJ/standard m^3^. It excludes landfill
      gas, digester gas, refinery gas, blast furnace gas, coke oven gas
      or gas derived through industrial processes from petroleum coke or
      coal, including synthetic gas. ([gaz naturel]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[on-site transportation emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[on-site transportation emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      emissions from registered or unregistered vehicles and other
      machinery that are used at the facility for the transport of
      substances, materials, equipment or products used in a production
      process or for the transport of individuals. ([émissions liées au
      transport sur le site]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[PFC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[PFC]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the perfluorocarbons set out in
      in items 25 to 33 of Schedule 3 to the
      Act. ([PFC]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Quantification Methods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Quantification Methods]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document
      entitled Quantification Methods for the Output-Based Pricing
      System Regulations, published by the Department of the Environment
      in 2022. ([méthodes de quantification]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[solid fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[solid fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fossil fuel that is
      solid at a temperature of 15°C and a pressure of
      101.325kPa. ([combustible solide]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[speciality lime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[speciality lime]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means lime produced by
      passing dolomitic lime through a kiln more than once or by adding
      material to dolomitic lime to change its properties. ([chaux
      spécialisée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified emission type]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified emission type]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an emission
      type listed in subsection 5(1). ([type d'émissions
      visé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specified industrial activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specified industrial activity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, with
      respect to a covered facility, an industrial activity referred to
      in subsection 5(2).  ([activité industrielle
      visée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stationary fuel combustion emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stationary fuel combustion emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      emissions from stationary devices that combust solid fuels, liquid
      fuels, gaseous fuels, or tires or asphalt shingles, whether in
      whole or in part, for the purpose of producing useful
      heat. ([émissions de combustion stationnaire de
      combustible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[thermal energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[thermal energy]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means useful thermal energy
      in the form of steam or hot water that is intended to be used for
      an industrial purpose. ([énergie thermique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[thermal energy to electricity ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[thermal energy to electricity ratio]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means,
      in respect of a unit or equipment that generates electricity, the
      ratio of the total quantity of thermal energy produced to the
      total quantity of gross electricity generated by the unit or
      equipment, not including the quantities from the use of duct
      burners, in a calendar year and expressed in the same units of
      measurement. ([rapport énergie
      thermique-électricité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[total capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[total capacity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, in respect of a unit
      or equipment that generates electricity, either

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the maximum continuous rating (the maximum net
        power that can be continuously sustained by a unit or equipment
        that generates electricity without the use of duct burners, at a
        temperature of 15˚C and a pressure of 101.325 kPa), expressed in
        MW of electricity, as most recently reported to a provincial
        authority of competent jurisdiction or to the electric system
        operator in the province where the unit or equipment is located,
        or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no report has been made, the most
        electricity that was generated by the unit or equipment during
        two continuous hours in a calendar year, expressed in MW of
        electricity. ([capacité totale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[2020 GHGRP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[2020 GHGRP]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2023-240, s.
      2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an assembly comprised of a
      boiler or combustion engine and any other equipment that is
      physically connected to either, including duct burners and other
      combustion devices, heat recovery systems, steam turbines,
      generators and emission control devices, and that generates
      electricity and, if applicable, produces thermal energy from the
      combustion of fossil fuels. ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[venting emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[venting emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means controlled
      emissions that occur due to the design of a facility, to
      procedures used in the manufacture or processing of a substance or
      product or to pressure exceeding the capacity of the equipment at
      the facility. ([émissions d'évacuation]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[waste emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[waste emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means emissions that
      result from waste disposal at a facility, including the
      landfilling of solid waste, the biological treatment or
      incineration of waste and the flaring of landfill gas. Waste
      emissions do not include emissions from the combustion of tires or
      asphalt shingles, whether in whole or in part, to produce useful
      heat or on-site transportation emissions. ([émissions des
      déchets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[wastewater emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[wastewater emissions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means emissions
      resulting from industrial wastewater and industrial wastewater
      treatment at a facility. ([émissions des eaux
      usées]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[WCI Method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[WCI Method]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2023-240, s.
      2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[white cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[white cement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mix composed
      primarily of clinker that contains 0.5% or less by weight of
      ferric oxide, gypsum and limestone. ([ciment
      blanc]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Unless otherwise indicated, a reference to any
  document incorporated by reference into these Regulations is a
  reference to the document as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accreditation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), if ISO Standard 14065 is
  amended, the previous version of the document may be complied with for
  a period of four years beginning on the day on which the amended
  version is published.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantification Methods

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), the version of Quantification
  Methods to be complied with for a compliance period is the version
  that was most recently published prior to the day on which that
  compliance period begins.

## [Purpose]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184062 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations implement
an output-based pricing system for industrial GHG emissions with respect
to covered facilities where industrial activities are engaged in.

## [Overview]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184065 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}System components

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** These Regulations set out

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which a person responsible for a
  covered facility must, under Part 2 of the Act, provide a report that
  sets out the information with respect to the GHG emissions limit and
  cause the report to be verified;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the methods to quantify the GHGs from a covered
  facility and the production from each specified industrial activity
  engaged in at the covered facility;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner of determining the covered facility's GHG
  emissions limit based on the facility's production from each specified
  industrial activity and the applicable output-based standard; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the manner in which compensation is provided for
  excess emissions and surplus credits are issued.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184072 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantification of GHGs

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to section 22, GHGs must be quantified for the following emission
  types:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} stationary fuel combustion emissions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} industrial process emissions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} industrial product use emissions;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} venting emissions;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} flaring emissions;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} leakage emissions;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} on-site transportation emissions;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} waste emissions; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} wastewater emissions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specified industrial activities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Output-based standards are established under these
  Regulations for the industrial activities set out in column 1 of
  Schedule 1 and for additional industrial activities engaged in at the
  covered facility.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 3](#1427351-1427774){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427351-1427774.html"}
:::

Version](section-5-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

## [End of designation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184092 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Condition not met

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  covered facility was designated on the condition that it would emit a
  quantity of GHGs of at least 10 kt of CO~2~e in any of the three
  calendar years following the date of first production, the Minister
  may cancel the designation under subsection 172(3) of the Act if the
  facility has not met that condition as of December 31 of the third
  calendar year following that date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must provide notice of their intention
  to cancel the covered facility's designation to the person responsible
  for the covered facility at least 30 days before cancelling the
  designation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 2](#1315318-1336205){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315318-1336205.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation following request

**[[6.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6.1 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister
receives a request to cancel a designation of a covered facility during
a calendar year and the Minister decides, under subsection 172(3) of the
Act, to cancel the designation, that cancellation is effective as of
December 31 of the calendar year in which the decision is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 4](#1427353-1427778){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427353-1427778.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ceasing to be a covered facility

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  facility ceases to be a covered facility under the following
  circumstances:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it has ceased production from all specified
    industrial activities for five consecutive compliance periods; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person responsible for the covered facility
    makes a request to that effect because it is expected that the
    specified industrial activities engaged in at the covered facility
    will cease for at least 12 consecutive months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of cessation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A covered facility ceases to be a covered facility on
  the following date:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), on December
    31 of the fifth consecutive calendar year for which no production is
    reported in an annual report; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), on the later
    of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the 30th day following the date the Minister
      receives the request from the person responsible for the covered
      facility, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date the covered facility ceases production.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2021-197, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 3](#1315320-1316088){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315320-1316088.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement for partial compliance period

**[[7.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.1 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 7.2,
if a facility ceases to be a covered facility during a compliance
period, the person who is responsible for that facility must comply with
these Regulations and the requirements of Division 1 of Part 2 of the
Act in respect of the portion of that compliance period during which it
was a covered facility.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 4](#1315321-1316092){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315321-1316092.html"}
- [SOR/2025-108, s. 2](#1516608-1523631){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1516608-1523631.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-7.1-20210812.html "Link to previous version of section 7.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

**[[7.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7.2 .sectionLabel}** Section 7.1 does not
apply in respect of a facility that ceases to be a covered facility
because the Minister cancelled its designation in 2025 under subsection
172(3) of the Act.

## [Covered Facility]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184111 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criteria --- definition section 169 of
Act

**[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph (a) of the definition [[covered
facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 169 of the Act, the
following criteria must be met by a facility that is located in a
province or area that is set out in Part 2 of Schedule 1 to the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a report was made, in accordance with a Notice with
  respect to reporting of greenhouse gases (GHGs) published under
  section 46 of the [Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
  1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31), in respect of that facility indicating that
  that facility emitted a quantity of GHGs equal to 50kt or more of
  CO~2~e, as one or more facilities as defined in such a notice, for the
  2014 calendar year or any subsequent calendar year; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the primary activity engaged in at the facility is
  any of the industrial activities set out in column 1 of Schedule 1.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 5](#1427355-1427781){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427355-1427781.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Remote community

**[[8.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8.1 .sectionLabel}** Unless designated by
the Minister as a covered facility under subsection 172(1) of the Act,
an electricity generation facility is not included in the definition
[[covered facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 169 of the Act if
the industrial activity set out in paragraph 38(b) or (c), column 1, of
Schedule 1 is engaged in at that facility and that facility

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} generates electricity for general distribution to the
  public in a geographic area that is neither serviced by an electrical
  network that distributes electricity and is subject to the standards
  of the North American Electric Reliability Corporation nor by a
  distribution system,

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not connected to an electrical network, for the
  distribution of electricity, that is subject to the standards of the
  North American Electric Reliability Corporation, and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} is not connected to a distribution system.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 6](#1427357-1427788){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427357-1427788.html"}
:::

## [Compliance Period]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184118 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance period

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsections (2) and (3), the specified period, for the purposes of
  the definition [[compliance period]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section
  169 of the Act, begins on January 1 and ends on December 31 for each
  calendar year, starting in 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}First compliance period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a facility becomes a covered facility under the
  Act after January 1 of a given calendar year, its specified period,
  for the purposes of the definition [[compliance
  period]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 169 of the Act, begins on
  January 1 of the calendar year following

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the calendar year during which the facility is
    registered under subsection 171(2) of the Act, if the facility meets
    the criteria set out in section 8, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the calendar year during which the facility was
    designated as a covered facility under subsection 172(1) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance period --- 2025

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the 2025 calendar year, if a facility ceases to
  be a covered facility because the Minister cancels its designation in
  2025 under subsection 172(3) of the Act, the specified period in
  respect of that facility, for the purposes of the definition
  [[compliance period]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in section 169 of the Act,
  begins on January 1, 2025 and ends on March 31, 2025.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 5](#1315323-1336211){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315323-1336211.html"}
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 7](#1427359-1427793){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427359-1427793.html"}
- [SOR/2025-108, s. 3](#1516610-1523638){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1516610-1523638.html"}
:::

## [Person Responsible]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184126 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person responsible

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, the person responsible for a facility or a covered facility
is the person who owns or is otherwise responsible for the facility or
covered facility, including the person who has the charge, management or
control of the facility or covered facility, or who is the true decision
maker with respect to the operations of the facility or covered
facility.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conclusive presumption

- **[[10.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  a facility ceases to be a covered facility, the person responsible for
  that facility is deemed to be responsible for a covered facility with
  respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirements set out in section 173 of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirements set out in section 174 of the Act
    with respect to providing compensation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirement under subsection 176(1) of the Act
    to notify the Minister of any errors or omissions;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the requirement under subsection 176(2) or 177(2)
    of the Act to submit any corrected report under section 62 or 63,
    respectively;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the requirement under paragraph 178(1)(a) of the
    Act to provide any compensation;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the requirements set out in section 181 of the Act;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the requirement to maintain the OBPS account
    referred to in section 14;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the requirement under subsection 187(5) of the Act
    to retain the information set out in section 47; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the requirement to notify the Minister if there is
    a change in the person responsible, in accordance with section 48.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requirements set out in paragraphs (1)(a) to (g)
  apply during the period necessary under the Act and the requirements
  set out in paragraphs (1)(h) and (i) apply during a period of seven
  years after the end of the year in which the facility ceased to be a
  covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surplus credits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a facility ceases to be a covered facility, the
  person responsible for that facility is deemed to be responsible for a
  covered facility for the purpose of obtaining surplus credits under
  section 175 of the Act and paragraph 178(1)(b) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 6](#1315325-1316110){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315325-1316110.html"}

## [Annual Report]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184129 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of annual report

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  section 16, the report that must be submitted by the person
  responsible for a covered facility for a compliance period under
  section 173 of the Act is prepared annually for each covered facility
  for which they are responsible and includes the information listed in
  Schedule 2 and the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a covered facility, other than one
    referred to in paragraph (b) or (c),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of GHGs from the covered
      facility during the compliance period, expressed in CO~2~e tonnes,
      as determined in accordance with section 17, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the covered facility's production during the
      compliance period, from each specified industrial activity,
      quantified in accordance with section 31;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an electricity generation facility,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of GHGs from each unit within
      the covered facility during the compliance period, expressed in
      CO~2~e tonnes, as determined in accordance with section 20,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the sum of the total quantities of GHGs
      determined under subparagraph (i) for all units within the
      facility, expressed in CO~2~e tonnes,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the production during the compliance period by
      each unit within the covered facility from each of the industrial
      activities set out in paragraphs 38(a) to (c), column 1, of
      Schedule 1 that are engaged in at the unit, separately for each
      industrial activity, quantified in accordance with section 32, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the sum of the production from all of the units
      within the covered facility during the compliance period, from all
      of the industrial activities set out in paragraphs 38(a) to (c),
      column 1, of Schedule 1;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a covered facility, where the
    specified industrial activities are both the production of coal by
    mining coal deposits and, if composed of a unit or a group of units
    that are registered under the Reduction of Carbon Dioxide Emissions
    from Coal-fired Generation of Electricity Regulations, the
    generation of electricity,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the total quantity of GHGs from the covered
      facility during the compliance period, which is the sum of the
      total quantity of GHGs from the mining of coal deposits,
      determined in accordance with section 17, and the total quantities
      of GHGs from the generation of electricity, determined in
      accordance with section 20, expressed in CO~2~e tonnes,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with respect to the production of coal by mining
      coal deposits, the production during the compliance period from
      each specified industrial activity, in accordance with section 31,
      and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} with respect to the generation of electricity,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the production during the compliance period by
        each unit within the covered facility from each of the
        industrial activities set out in paragraphs 38(a) to (c), column
        1, of Schedule 1 that are engaged in at the unit, separately for
        each industrial activity, in accordance with section 32, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the sum of the production from all of the units
        within the covered facility during the compliance period, from
        all of the industrial activities set out in paragraphs 38(a) to
        (c), column 1, of Schedule 1;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of GHGs emitted from the covered
    facility during the compliance period as determined in accordance
    with section 35, and, if captured and stored CO~2~ is being
    subtracted under that section, the total quantity of CO~2~ captured
    and stored in a storage project that meets the criteria in
    subsection 35(2);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the GHG emissions limit applicable to the covered
    facility for the compliance period, calculated

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a facility referred to in paragraph
      (a), in accordance with section 36, 36.1 or 36.2,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a facility referred to in
      paragraph (b), in accordance with section 41, 41.1 or 41.2, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a facility referred to in
      paragraph (c), in accordance with section 42; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the positive result (corresponding to the quantity
    of GHGs emitted in excess of the emissions limit) or negative result
    (corresponding to the difference between the quantity of GHGs
    emitted and the emissions limit and indicating emissions below the
    emissions limit) obtained under section 44 for the compliance
    period, expressed in CO~2~e tonnes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increased electricity generation
  capacity

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(a)(ii), if
  section 36.2 applies with respect to a covered facility, the annual
  report must include the gross quantity of electricity generated that
  is attributed the capacity added to the equipment and gross quantity
  of electricity generated that is attributed to the capacity of the
  equipment before the additional capacity was added, separately,
  quantified in accordance with section 31 and subsection 36.2(3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increased electricity generation
  capacity

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraphs 1(b)(iii)
  and (iv) and (c)(iii), if section 41.2 applies with respect to an
  electricity generation facility, the annual report must include,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each unit whose electricity generation capacity
    from gaseous fuels was increased by 50 MW or more and that is
    designed to operate at a thermal energy to electricity ratio of less
    than 0.9, the gross amount of electricity generated during the
    compliance period by each unit that is attributed to the capacity
    added to the unit and the gross amount of electricity generated that
    is attributed to the capacity of the unit before the additional
    capacity was added, in accordance with section 32 and subsection
    41.2(3), separately; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sum, from all of the units referred to in
    paragraph (a), of the gross amount of electricity generated that is
    attributed to the capacity added to the units and of the gross
    amount of electricity generated that is attributed to the capacity
    of the units before the additional capacity was added, separately.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- new covered facilities

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(e) and (f) do not apply with respect
  to a report that must be submitted by the person responsible for a
  covered facility for which sections 36 to 42 do not apply under
  section 43.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New additional industrial activity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraph (1)(a)(ii), an
  additional industrial activity that was recognized by the Minister
  during a calendar year is not taken into account for the annual report
  for the compliance period that corresponds to that calendar year.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 7](#1315327-1336214){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315327-1336214.html"}
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 9](#1427363-1427798){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427363-1427798.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20220101.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional content -- thermal energy

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  person responsible for a covered facility sells thermal energy that is
  produced at the covered facility to other covered facilities or buys
  thermal energy from any other covered facility, they must include in
  their annual report

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of thermal energy, expressed in
    gigajoules, as well as the thermal energy's temperature and
    pressure,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} sold to another covered facility during the
      compliance period, as determined by the quantity of thermal energy
      on sales receipts or by another objective method, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} bought from another covered facility during the
      compliance period, as determined by the quantity of thermal energy
      on sales receipts or by another objective method; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the ratio of heat from the combustion of fossil
    fuel to produce that thermal energy, calculated in accordance with
    section 34.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional content -- gypsum products

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person responsible for a covered facility where
  the specified industrial activity set out in item 10, column 1, of
  Schedule 1 is engaged in, must include in their annual report, the
  quantity, in tonnes, of each gypsum product that contains at least 70
  weight percent of calcium sulphate dihydrate produced during the
  compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional content -- hydrogen gas

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a covered facility where a specified industrial
  activity set out in items 2, 3, 15 or 29, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
  engaged in produces hydrogen gas, the person responsible for the
  covered facility must include in their annual report the quantity of
  hydrogen gas produced during the compliance period, in tonnes, and the
  quantity of hydrogen gas sold during the compliance period, in tonnes.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of annual report

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  person responsible for a covered facility must submit their annual
  report to the Minister, on or before June 1 of the calendar year
  following the end of the compliance period for which the annual report
  is prepared, along with a verification report prepared in accordance
  with section 52.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of errors or omissions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person responsible for a covered facility must
  correct the errors or omissions, identified by a verification body
  during the verification of the annual report, prior to submitting the
  annual report to the Minister, if possible.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-114, s. 1](#1247781-1249741){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1247781-1249741.html"}
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 10](#1427365-1427801){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427365-1427801.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-13-20200531.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Account opening

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** The account that the
person responsible for the covered facility opens in accordance with
subsection 186(1) of the Act is an Output-Based Pricing System account
(OBPS account).

## [Request for Confidentiality]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184176 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content of request

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** A request for
confidentiality submitted for the purposes of section 254 of the Act
must provide the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information to which the request pertains,
  clearly identified;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reason for the request from among those specified
  in paragraphs 254(a) to (c) of the Act; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the supporting justification that the information
  referred to in paragraph (a) has been treated as confidential by the
  person making the request and is not, and has never been, available to
  the public.

## [Quantification]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184182 .Part}

### [Variation of General Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184183 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Production of petrochemical products as a
by-product

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  production of a petrochemical product set out in item 17, column 1, of
  Schedule 1 as a by-product, at a covered facility where an industrial
  activity, other than one set out in that item is engaged in, is not an
  industrial activity covered by item 17, column 1, of Schedule 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of natural gas
  liquids

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If natural gas liquids are produced at a covered
  facility where a specified industrial activity set out in item 3 or
  17, column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of natural gas liquids in accordance with the methods
    applicable to the industrial activity set out in item 3 or 17, as
    the case may be, column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in item 4, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
    deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of hydrogen gas

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If hydrogen gas is produced at a covered facility
  where a specified industrial activity set out in item 2, 3, 15 or 29,
  column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of hydrogen gas in accordance with the method applicable
    to the industrial activity set out in item 2, 3, 15 or 29, as the
    case may be, column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activities set out in items 6 and 17, column 1, of
    Schedule 1 are deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of metal tubes

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If metal tubes are produced at a covered facility
  where a specified industrial activity set out in item 19 or 20, column
  1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of metal tubes in accordance with the method applicable
    to the industrial activity set out in item 19 or 20, as the case may
    be, column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in item 22, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
    deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of lime

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If lime is produced at a covered facility where a
  specified industrial activity set out in item 20, column 1, of
  Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of lime in accordance with the method applicable to the
    industrial activity set out in item 20, column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in item 8, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
    deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of electricity

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If electricity is produced at a covered facility
  where a specified industrial activity set out in item 20, column 1, of
  Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    generation of electricity in accordance with the method applicable
    to the industrial activity set out in item 20, column 1, of Schedule
    1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 36, the industrial
    activity set out in item 38, column 1, of Schedule 1 is deemed not
    to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of evaporated
  salt

  [(6.1)]{.lawlabel} If evaporated salt is produced through solution
  mining at a covered facility where a specified industrial activity set
  out in item 24, column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of evaporated salt in accordance with the method
    applicable to the industrial activity set out in item 24, column 1,
    of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in item 24.1, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
    deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrometallurgical smelting of zinc and
  lead

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If zinc and lead are pyrometallurgically smelted at a
  covered facility where a specified industrial activity set out in
  paragraph 23(b), column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    pyrometallurgical smelting of zinc and lead in accordance with the
    method applicable to the industrial activity set out in paragraph
    23(b), column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in paragraph 23(c), column 1, of
    Schedule 1 is deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pyrometallurgical smelting and refining
  of lead

  [(7.1)]{.lawlabel} If lead is pyrometallurgically smelted and refined
  at a covered facility where a specified industrial activity set out in
  paragraph 23(c), column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    pyrometallurgical smelting and refining of lead in accordance with
    the method applicable to the industrial activity set out in
    paragraph 23(c), column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in paragraph 23(b), column 1, of
    Schedule 1 is deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of precious
  metals

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If gold, silver, platinum or palladium is produced at
  a covered facility where a specified industrial activity set out in
  paragraph 26(d), column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of those metals in accordance with the method applicable
    to the industrial activity set out in paragraph 26(d), column 1, of
    Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in paragraph 26(c) or (f), column 1, of
    Schedule 1 is deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of petrochemicals

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (9.1), if a petrochemical
  product referred to in item 17, column 1, of Schedule 1 is produced at
  a covered facility where a specified industrial activity set out in
  item 3 or 4, column 1, of that Schedule is engaged in, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of that petrochemical in accordance with the method
    applicable to the industrial activity set out in item 3 or 4, as the
    case may be, column 1, of Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in item 17, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
    deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Parallel production

  [(9.1)]{.lawlabel} If a covered facility has at least one refinery
  that is engaged in a specified industrial activity set out in item 3,
  column 1, of Schedule 1, and one petrochemical plant, that is engaged
  in a specified industrial activity referred to in item 17, column 1,
  of Schedule 1, subsection (9) only applies to the refinery.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional production of precious
  metals

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} If silver, platinum or palladium is produced at a
  covered facility where a specified industrial activity set out in
  paragraph 26(f), column 1, of Schedule 1 is engaged in, the following
  rules apply:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of section 17, the person
    responsible for the covered facility must quantify the GHGs from the
    production of those metals in accordance with the method applicable
    to the industrial activity set out in paragraph 26(f), column 1, of
    Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of sections 31, 36 and 36.2, the
    industrial activity set out in paragraph 26(c), column 1, of
    Schedule 1 is deemed not to be engaged in at the covered facility.

### [Quantification of GHGs]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184223 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Total GHGs

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (5) and
  section 18, the total quantity of GHGs from a covered facility other
  than an electricity generation facility, during a compliance period,
  expressed in CO~2~e tonnes, is determined by the formula

  ::: {#1184227 .Subsection}
  <figure>
  <div class="imageGroup">
  <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_001e.png"
  alt="The summation of the products of Ej and GWPj for each greenhouse gas type “j”, summed for all specified emissions types “i”" />
  </div>
  </figure>

  where

  [E~j~]{.dfn}
  :   is the quantity of each GHG type "j" from the covered facility
      during a compliance period, for each specified emission type,
      determined in accordance with subsections (2) to (4);

  [GWP~j~]{.dfn}
  :   is the global warming potential of the GHG type "j" applicable to
      the compliance period and, if it is used to determine the
      quantities referred to in the descriptions of A, C and F in
      subsection 37(1), for the reference year "i", the global warming
      potential applicable to the compliance period in respect of which
      the output-based standard is being calculated;

  [i]{.dfn}
  :   is the i^th^ specified emission type, where "i" goes from 1 to n
      and where n is the number of the facility's specified emission
      types; and

  [j]{.dfn}
  :   is the j^th^ GHG type, where "j" goes from 1 to m and where m is
      the number of greenhouse gases.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of each GHG

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of a GHG type "j" from a covered
  facility during a compliance period for a specified emission type "i"
  is the sum of the following quantities, determined in accordance with
  the applicable requirements set out in Quantification Methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantities of that GHG from industrial
    activities set out in column 1 of Schedule 1 and set out in column 2
    of the table to the Part of Schedule 3 that is applicable to those
    industrial activities, for a specified emission type set out in
    column 1 of that table;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the quantities of that GHG from those industrial
    activities, but not set out in column 2 of the table to the Part of
    Schedule 3 that applies to those activities or for a specified
    emission type not set out in column 1 of that table; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any quantity of that GHG that is not referred to in
    paragraph (a) or (b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sampling, analysis and measurement
  requirements

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the quantity of a GHG is determined in accordance
  with subsection (2), the sampling, analysis and measurement
  requirements that apply are those set out in Quantification Methods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Missing data

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), if, for any
  reason beyond the control of the person responsible for a covered
  facility, the data required to quantify the GHGs from a covered
  facility are missing for a given period of a compliance period,
  replacement data for the given period must be calculated in accordance
  with Quantification Methods.

- [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2023-240, s. 12\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Biomass --- exclusion of CH~4~ and
  N~2~O

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the determination made under
  subsection (1), the quantities of CH~4~ and N~2~O generated from
  stationary devices that combust biomass for the purpose of producing
  useful heat are subtracted from the quantities of CH~4~ and N~2~O
  calculated in accordance with subsections (2) to (4) for stationary
  fuel combustion emissions.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional generation of electricity

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 17, the quantities of the GHGs for specified emission types from
the generation of electricity using fossil fuels by a covered facility
--- other than a covered facility referred to in paragraph 11(1)(c) ---
are determined in accordance with the methods that are applicable to any
of the industrial activities engaged in at the covered facility.

  ajax-after="1427381-1437688.html"}
:::

:::

SOR/2023-240, s. 13\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Total emissions per unit --- electricity

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), with
  respect to an electricity generation facility, the total quantity of
  GHGs from each unit within a facility, during a compliance period,
  expressed in CO~2~e tonnes, is determined by the formula

  ::: {#1184262 .Subsection}
  <figure>
  <div class="imageGroup">
  <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_001e.png"
  alt="The summation of the products of Ej and GWPj for each greenhouse gas type “j”, summed for all specified emissions types “i”" />
  </div>
  </figure>

  where

  [E~j~]{.dfn}
  :   is the quantity of each GHG type "j" from the unit during a
      compliance period for each specified emission type determined in
      accordance with subsections (2) to (5);

  [GWP~j~]{.dfn}
  :   is the global warming potential of the GHG type "j" applicable to
      the compliance period;

  [i]{.dfn}
  :   is the i^th^ specified emission type, where "i" goes from 1 to n
      and where n is the unit's number of specified emission types;

  [j]{.dfn}
  :   is the j^th^ GHG type, where "j" goes from 1 to m and where m is
      the number of greenhouse gases.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Quantity of each GHG

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of a GHG type "j" generated by a unit
  during a compliance period for a specified emission type "i" is the
  sum of the following quantities, determined in accordance with the
  applicable requirements set out in Quantification Methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantities of the GHG that is set out in column
    2 of the table to Part 38 of Schedule 3 for a specified emission
    type set out in column 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any quantity of the GHG that is not referred to in
    paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Apportioning GHGs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (2)(b) or (c), if the
  GHGs for a specified emission type referred to in subsection (2) can
  only be quantified for the facility as a whole, the quantity of those
  GHGs must be apportioned to the facility's units on the basis of each
  unit's total generation of electricity relative to the facility's
  total generation of electricity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sampling, analysis and measurement
  requirements

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the quantity of a GHG is determined in accordance
  with subsection (2), the sampling, analysis and measurement
  requirements that apply are those set out in Quantification Methods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Missing data

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), if, for any
  reason beyond the control of the person responsible for a covered
  facility, the data required to quantify GHGs generated by a unit are
  missing for a given period of a compliance period, replacement data
  for the given period must be calculated in accordance with
  Quantification Methods.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Biomass --- exclusion of CH~4~ and
  N~2~O

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the determination made under
  subsection (1), the quantity of CH~4~ and N~2~O generated from
  stationary devices that combust biomass for the purpose of producing
  useful heat are subtracted from the quantity of CH~4~ and N~2~O
  calculated in accordance with subsections (2) to (5) for stationary
  fuel combustion emissions.

  ajax-after="1427383-1427807.html"}
:::

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hybrid configuration

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 20, if a combustion engine unit and a boiler unit share the same
steam turbine, the GHGs from those units are quantified as follows:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a combustion engine unit, the
  quantification provisions apply to the assembly comprised of
  combustion engines and any other equipment connected to them,
  including the steam turbine that it shares with the boiler unit; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with respect to a boiler unit, the quantification
  provisions apply to the assembly comprised of boilers and any other
  equipment connected to them, including the steam turbine that it
  shares with the combustion engine unit.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Biomass --- exclusion of CO~2~

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} CO~2~
  from biomass is not included in the quantity of CO~2~ calculated in
  accordance with subsections 17(2) to (4) or 20(2) to (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Methane

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} CH~4~ from venting or leakage emissions from an
  industrial activity set out in item 1, 2, 4 or 5, column 1, of
  Schedule 1 is not included in the quantity of CH~4~ calculated in
  accordance with subsections 17(2) to (4).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measuring device

**[[22.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22.1 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise
provided for in a method set out in Quantification Methods, any
measuring device that is used to determine a quantity for the purposes
of these Regulations must

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be installed, operated, maintained and calibrated in
  accordance with the manufacturer's specifications, or, if those
  specifications are not available, with any applicable generally
  recognized national or international industry standard; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} maintain accuracy within plus or minus 5%.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 15](#1427390-1437709){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427390-1437709.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}De minimis

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  quantity of a GHG for any specified emission type does not need to be
  included in the determination made under subsections 17(2) to (4) or
  20(2) to (5) if the quantity of the GHG, expressed in CO~2~e tonnes,
  does not exceed 0.5% of the total quantity of GHGs determined under
  subsection 17(1) or 20(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The sum of the quantities of GHGs not included under
  subsection (1) cannot exceed 0.5% of the total quantity of GHGs
  determined under subsection 17(1) or 20(1).

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2021-197, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-24-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 24"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuous Emissions Monitoring System

**[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** If a continuous emissions
monitoring system is used to quantify GHGs under these Regulations, the
person responsible for the covered facility must ensure that the system
complies with the requirements set out in Quantification Methods.

:::

### [Permit To Use an Alternative Method]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184308 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative method

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** Despite sections 17 and
20, the person responsible for a covered facility may use a method other
than a method set out in Quantification Methods if they have a permit
issued in accordance with section 28.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 17](#1427394-1437725){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427394-1437725.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20200101.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for permit

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application for a permit must be submitted to the Minister and must
  contain the information referred to in Schedule 4.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must be accompanied by a
  certification, dated and signed by the person responsible for the
  covered facility or by their authorized official, stating that the
  information contained in the application is accurate and complete.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions of issuance

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must issue the permit to use a method of quantification other
  than the method set out in Quantification Methods if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person responsible for the covered facility
    establishes that, at the time of the application, it is not
    technically or economically feasible to use the method set out in
    Quantification Methods;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person responsible for the covered facility
    demonstrates that the method being proposed is at least as rigorous
    as the requirements set out in Quantification Methods and provides
    equivalent results to those that would have been obtained from those
    requirements;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the person responsible for the covered facility
    provides a plan describing measures that will be taken to enable the
    use of the method set out in Quantification Methods and the
    implementation period for that plan, up to a maximum of two years;
    and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the requested term of the permit does not exceed
    the period for which the permit is necessary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period of validity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The term of the permit must not exceed 24 months.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds for refusing permit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must refuse to issue a permit if the
  Minister has reasonable grounds to believe that the applicant has
  provided false or misleading information in support of their
  application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The permit can only be renewed once.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 18](#1427396-1437732){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427396-1437732.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20200101.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for renewal

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  application for the renewal of a permit must include the information
  referred to in Schedule 4 and an explanation of the reasons why the
  plan that was submitted in the initial permit application was not
  implemented within the period identified in the initial application.
  The application for renewal must be submitted to the Minister at least
  90 days before the expiration of the permit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for renewal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must renew the permit if the conditions
  set out in subsection 28(1) are met.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds for revocation

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister must revoke the permit if the Minister has reasonable grounds
  to believe that the permit holder has provided false or misleading
  information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of revocation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before revoking a permit, the Minister must provide
  the permit holder with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} written reasons for the revocation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an opportunity to make written representations in
    respect of the revocation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of revocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The revocation of a permit is effective 30 days after
  the day on which the Minister notifies the permit holder.

### [Quantification of Production for Specified Industrial Activities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184343 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}General rule

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4) and
  section 16, the production from a covered facility, other than an
  electricity generation facility, from each specified industrial
  activity during a compliance period is quantified

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of production from a specified
    industrial activity set out in items 1 to 37 and 39 to 44, column 1,
    of Schedule 1, in the units of measurement set out in column 2 of
    Schedule 1 for that industrial activity, and in accordance with any
    requirements set out in the applicable part of Schedule 3;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of production from a specified
    industrial activity set out in paragraphs 38(a) to (c), column 1, of
    Schedule 1, the production is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} quantified in whole for the compliance period in
      accordance with the requirements set out in sections 3 and 4 of
      Part 38 of Schedule 3, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} not quantified, in whole or in part, for the
      compliance period; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of production from an additional
    industrial activity, in the units of measurement specified by the
    Minister for that activity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Measuring device

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any measuring device that is used to determine a
  quantity for the purposes of these Regulations must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be installed, operated, maintained and calibrated
    in accordance with the manufacturer's specifications or, if those
    specifications are not available, any applicable generally
    recognized national or international industry standard; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} maintain accuracy within plus or minus 5%.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engineering estimates or mass balance

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If it is not possible to directly measure production
  using a measuring device, it may be quantified using engineering
  estimates or mass balance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional provision

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the 2019 calendar year

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the production quantified in accordance with
    paragraphs (1)(a) and (c), must be accurate within ± 5%; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the production referred to in paragraph (1)(b) may
    be quantified by the person responsible for the regulated facility
    in accordance with subsection 9(2) and section 103 of the
    [Greenhouse Gas Emissions Information Production
    Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read before August 1,
    2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 19](#1427398-1427810){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427398-1427810.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20231109.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electricity generation facility

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the gross
  electricity generated during a compliance period by each unit within
  the electricity generation facility, from each of the industrial
  activities set out in paragraphs 38(a) to (c), column 1, of Schedule 1
  that is engaged in at the unit, is determined as follows:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a unit uses only one fossil fuel to generate
    electricity, in accordance with subsection 1(1) and section 2 of
    Part 38 of Schedule 3; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a unit uses a mixture of fossil fuels or a
    mixture of biomass and fossil fuels to generate electricity, in
    accordance with subsections 1(2) and (3) and section 2 of Part 38 of
    Schedule 3.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Choose not to quantify

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person responsible for the electricity generation
  facility may choose not to quantify part or all of the quantity of
  electricity generated from one unit or a group of units.
