### [Compensation and Issuance of Surplus Credits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184777 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excess emissions

**[[54]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-54 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 174(1) of the Act, if, during a compliance period, a covered
facility emits GHGs in a quantity that exceeds the applicable emissions
limit, the compensation to be provided by the person responsible for
that facility is to be established based on the quantity of GHGs,
expressed in CO~2~e tonnes, that was emitted in excess of the emissions
limit as reported in the annual report submitted for the compliance
period or the Minister's determination under section 53, as the case may
be.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excess emissions charge payment

- **[[55]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-55 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  compensation that is provided by means of an excess emissions charge
  payment must be made electronically to the Receiver General for
  Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surplus credits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any compensation that is provided by means of a
  remittance of surplus credits must be made in the manner set out in
  section 70.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other compliance units

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any compensation that is provided by means of a
  remittance of compliance units other than surplus credits must be made
  in the manner set out in section 71.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum percentage --- charge

**[[56]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-56 .sectionLabel}** A minimum of 25% of the
compensation that is required under section 174 of the Act must be
provided by means of an excess emissions charge payment for each
compliance period beginning with the 2022 compliance period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 21](#1315376-1336445){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315376-1336445.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regular-rate compensation deadline

- **[[57]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-57 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 174(3) of the Act, the regular-rate
  compensation deadline is December 15 of the calendar year in which the
  related annual report must be submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), for the compliance period
  that ends on December 31, 2019, the regular-rate compensation deadline
  is April 15, 2021.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increased-rate compensation deadline

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 174(4) of the Act, the
  increased-rate compensation deadline is February 15 of the calendar
  year following the deadline under subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), for the compliance period
  that ends on December 31, 2019, the increased-rate compensation
  deadline is June 15, 2021.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice referred to in subsection 53(3)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} However, if a notice of determination under
  subsection 53(3) is issued after October 31 of the year in which the
  related annual report is due

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the regular-rate compensation deadline is 45 days
    after the day on which the notice is issued; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the increased-rate compensation deadline is 60 days
    after the day on which the regular-rate compensation deadline under
    paragraph (a) expires.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-114, s. 2](#1247783-1249747){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1247783-1249747.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-57-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 57"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compensation information

**[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** A person that is
responsible for a covered facility must, at the time compensation is
provided, submit to the Minister the following information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the covered facility certificate number;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the provision of the Act under which the compensation
  is being provided;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the compliance period for which compensation is being
  provided;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the quantity of GHGs emitted in excess of the
  emissions limit in respect of which the compensation is provided,
  expressed in CO~2~e tonnes;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} the details of any excess emissions charge payment,
  including

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the amount in dollars paid to the Receiver General
    for Canada,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the applicable rate, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date of payment;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the details of any surplus credits or offset credits
  remitted, including, for each type of credit,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number remitted,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance transaction,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the remittance transaction number,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the serial numbers, and

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the date or dates on which they were issued; and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the details of any units or credits, recognized as
  compliance units, remitted, including

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number remitted,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the province or program authority referred to in
    subsection 78(1) that issued the units or credits,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the date of their retirement or the date they are
    designated for use exclusively as a compliance unit for the purposes
    of remittance to the Minister under section 174, paragraph 178(1)(a)
    or subsection 181(2) of the Act, as the case may be,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the serial numbers assigned to them by the
    province or program authority referred to in subsection 78(1),

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the start date of the offset project for which the
    unit or credit was issued,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the year in which the GHG reduction or removal
    occurred for which the unit or credit was issued,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the offset protocol applicable to the project for
    which the unit or credit was issued, including the version number
    and publication date, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the accredited verification body
    that verified the units or credits.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 32](#1427453-1427933){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427453-1427933.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-58-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 58"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surplus credits

- **[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 175 of the Act and subject to subsection (2),
  the number of surplus credits, equivalent to the difference between
  the emissions limit and the quantity of GHGs emitted from the covered
  facility, that the Minister issues is based on what is reported in the
  annual report submitted for the compliance period if the emissions
  limit that was set out in the report was calculated in accordance with
  these Regulations, unless a material discrepancy within the meaning of
  subsection 49(2) exists with respect to the total quantity of GHGs or
  the production from one of the specified industrial activities that is
  used in the calculation of the emissions limit for the compliance
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister will not issue surplus credits if the
  Minister has established the emissions limit or determined the
  quantity of GHGs emitted from the covered facility for the compliance
  period under section 53.

### [Errors and Omissions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184825 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification by person responsible

**[[61]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-61 .sectionLabel}** The notice submitted
under subsection 176(1) of the Act by a person responsible for a covered
facility who has become aware of an error or omission in an annual
report must indicate whether

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the error or omission would have constituted a
  material discrepancy under subsection 49(2) if it had been identified
  during the verification of the annual report; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the aggregate of all errors and omissions would have
  constituted a material discrepancy under subsection 49(2) if it had
  been identified during the verification of the annual report.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrected report

- **[[62]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-62 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  notice indicated that the error or omission, or the aggregate of all
  errors and omissions, would have constituted a material discrepancy
  under subsection 49(2), a corrected report, along with a verification
  report prepared in accordance with section 52, must be submitted to
  the Minister by the person responsible within 120 days after the day
  on which the notice is submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The corrected report must include the information
  referred to in sections 11 and 12 and, under a heading, the following
  information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information provided in the annual report that
    required correction and a description of the corrections made;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the circumstances that led to the
    errors or omissions and an indication of the reasons why the error
    or omission was not previously detected; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a description of the measures that have been and
    will be implemented to avoid future errors or omissions of the same
    type.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 34](#1427457-1427949){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427457-1427949.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-62-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 62"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification by the Minister

- **[[63]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-63
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 62(2) applies to a report
  that is required by the Minister under subsection 177(2) of the Act
  and the report must be submitted to the Minister within the following
  period:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a corrected report, 60 days after
    the day on which the Minister required it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a verified corrected report, 120
    days after the day on which the Minister required it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Verification report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The corrected report submitted under paragraph (1)(b)
  must be submitted with a verification report prepared in accordance
  with section 52.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 35](#1427459-1427952){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427459-1427952.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-63-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 63"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change in obligations

- **[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of section 178 of the Act, the revised compensation to be
  paid or remitted or the number of surplus credits to be issued, as the
  case may be, is equal to the difference between the result of the
  assessment made in accordance with section 44, and reported in the
  annual report, and the result that is reported in the corrected
  report.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revised compensation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 178(1)(a) of the Act,
  any revised compensation is to be provided by means of an excess
  emissions charge payment or a remittance of compliance units. Revised
  compensation is to be provided if the difference referred to in
  subsection (1) is greater than or equal to 500 CO~2~e tonnes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance of surplus credits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph 178(1)(b) of the Act
  and subject to subsection (4), the Minister may issue a number of
  surplus credits that is equivalent to the difference between, as the
  case may be,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of surplus credits that corresponds to
    the result obtained under section 44 based on the corrected report
    and the number of surplus credits issued under section 175 of the
    Act based on the annual report; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicable emissions limit and the quantity of
    GHGs emitted from the covered facility that are reported in the
    corrected report, if the emissions limit that was set out in the
    corrected report was calculated in accordance with these
    Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister will not issue surplus credits if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a material discrepancy within the meaning of
    subsection 49(2) exists with respect to the total quantity of GHGs
    reported in the corrected report or the production from one of the
    specified industrial activities that is used in the calculation of
    the emissions limit reported in the corrected report for the
    compliance period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the emissions limit or quantity of GHGs emitted
    from the covered facility is determined by the Minister for the
    compliance period under section 53.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 36](#1427461-1427968){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427461-1427968.html"}
:::

SOR/2023-240, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excess Surplus credits issued

- **[[66]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-66 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  corrected report shows that an excess number of surplus credits was
  issued to the person responsible for a covered facility and the
  credits in question remain in an account in the tracking system that
  is linked to the covered facility, the Minister must revoke the excess
  credits without notice. The revocation is effective as of the date on
  which the corrected report is submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Insufficient surplus credits in account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any of the excess surplus credits are no longer in
  an account in the tracking system that is linked to the covered
  facility, the person responsible for the facility must, within the
  compensation deadlines set out in subsections 69(1) and (2), make up
  the amount owed by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} remitting to the Minister other surplus credits in
    accordance with section 70;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} remitting to the Minister other compliance units in
    accordance with section 71; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} making an excess emissions charge payment in
    accordance with section 55.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Charge

**[[67]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-67 .sectionLabel}** An excess emissions
charge payment made for the purposes of subsection 64(2) must be made in
the manner set out in section 55.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-240, s. 37](#1427463-1427972){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1427463-1427972.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-67-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 67"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surplus credits

- **[[68]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-68 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  revised compensation that is provided by the remittance of surplus
  credits must be made in the manner set out in section 70.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other compliance units

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any revised compensation that is provided by the
  remittance of compliance units other than surplus credits must be made
  in the manner set out in section 71.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regular-rate compensation deadline

- **[[69]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-69 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} With
  respect to revised compensation, the regular rate referred to in
  subsection 174(3) of the Act applies for a period of 45 days after the
  day on which the corrected report must be submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increased-rate compensation deadline

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If compensation is not provided in full by the
  deadline set out in subsection (1), the increased rate referred to in
  subsection 174(4) of the Act applies for a period of 60 days after
  that deadline.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other compensation deadlines

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a corrected report is submitted to the Minister in
  respect of a compliance period for which the regular-rate compensation
  deadline set out in subsection 57(1) or (1.1) has not expired, the
  compensation deadline in respect of that compliance period is the
  later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the compensation deadlines set out in subsection
    57(1), (1.1), (2) or (2.1), as the case may be, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the compensation deadlines under subsection (1) or
    (2), as the case may be.

### [Remittance of Compliance Units]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184900 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surplus credits

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), any
  surplus credit may be remitted to the Minister for the purposes of
  subsection 174(1) or paragraph 178(1)(a) of the Act if the credit was
  issued no more than five calendar years before the remittance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Details --- removal of province

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The surplus credit must have been issued in respect
  of a facility that is located in a province that was listed in Part 2
  of Schedule 1 to the Act for at least one day during the compliance
  period for which the credit is being remitted.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 23](#1315380-1336274){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315380-1336274.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-70-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 70"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other compliance units

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** Any recognized unit or
credit or offset credit may be remitted to the Minister for the purposes
of subsection 174(1) or paragraph 178(1)(a) of the Act if the unit or
credit, as the case may be, was issued for GHG reductions or removals
that occurred no more than eight calendar years before the remittance.

### [Suspension and Revocation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184905 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension --- reasonable grounds

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 180(1) of the Act, the Minister may suspend
  a surplus credit in an account if the Minister has reasonable grounds
  to believe that the credit

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} was already used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} was issued on the basis of false or misleading
    information; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is no longer valid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension --- removal of province

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 180(1) of the Act,
  the Minister may suspend all surplus credits

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that were issued in respect of a covered facility
    that is located in a province that was listed in Part 2 of Schedule
    1 to the Act but is no longer listed in that Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that are in an account in the tracking system that
    is linked to a facility that is located in a province that is no
    longer listed in Part 2 of Schedule 1 to the Act and ceased being a
    covered facility at the time when the name of the province in which
    it is located was removed from Part 2 of Schedule 1 to the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must, without delay, notify the holder
  of the account of the suspension of the credit, the reasons for the
  suspension and the date on which it takes effect.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Response

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a credit was suspended on any of the grounds
  listed in subsection (1), the holder of the account may, within 30
  days after the day on which the Minister's notice under subsection (2)
  is issued, submit to the Minister their reasons why the credit should
  not be suspended.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditional removal of suspension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The person responsible for a facility who holds an
  account that is linked to the facility in the tracking system in which
  there remain surplus credits that were suspended as a result of the
  name of a province being removed from Part 2 of Schedule 1 to the Act
  may make a request to the Minister to lift the suspension of those
  surplus credits if the surplus credits are to be provided as
  compensation for the facility's excess emissions under subsection
  174(1) of the Act or paragraph 178(1)(a) of the Act, in respect of a
  compliance period during which the facility was a covered facility.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 24](#1315382-1336286){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315382-1336286.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-72-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 72"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** The Minister must, after
the period set out in subsection 72(3), thoroughly review the reasons
for the suspension that are set out in subsection 72(1) and notify the
holder of the account that

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Minister determines that the reasons for the
  suspension are well-founded, the surplus credit is revoked; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the suspension of the credit is lifted.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-197, s. 25](#1315389-1336290){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1315389-1336290.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-73-20190628.html "Link to previous version of section 73"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for cancellation

**[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** A request under
subsection 180(2) of the Act to cancel a surplus credit or offset credit
must be made to the Minister in writing and include the serial number of
the credit to be cancelled.

### [Issuance Error or Invalidity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184922 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsection 181(1) of the
Act

- **[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the
  Minister requires a person, under subsection 181(1) of the Act, to
  remit a compliance unit, the Minister must provide the person a notice
  indicating the reason for the remittance, the number of compliance
  units to be remitted and the deadline by which the remittance is to be
  made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of remittance

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The compliance units remitted to the Minister under
  subsection 181(2) of the Act must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of surplus credits, have been issued
    within five calendar years before the deadline indicated in the
    notice provided under subsection (1) and have been issued in respect
    of a covered facility located in a province that was listed in Part
    2 of Schedule 1 to the Act for at least one day during the
    compliance period for which the credit is being remitted; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of recognized units or credits or
    offset credits, have been issued for GHG reductions or removals that
    occurred within the eight calendar years before the deadline
    indicated in the notice provided under subsection (1).

### [Tracking System]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1184930 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Accounts for participants

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 186(1) of the Act, any person, other than a person
responsible for a covered facility or a
[[proponent]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of subsection
1(1) of the [Canadian Greenhouse Gas Offset Credit System
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2022-111), who wishes to open an
account in the tracking system must notify the Minister in writing.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of closure

- **[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If an
  account, other than a GHG Offset Credit System account as defined in
  the [Canadian Greenhouse Gas Offset Credit System
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2022-111), has been inactive for
  more than seven years, the Minister may give 60 days' notice to the
  holder of the account of the Minister's intent to close the account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Closing of account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the holder of the account fails to request that
  the account remain open before the expiry of the 60 days, the Minister
  may close the account under subsection 186(3) of the Act.

## [Recognized Units or Credits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184938 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance unit

- **[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A unit
  or credit is to be recognized as a compliance unit if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is issued by a province or by a program
    authority on behalf of a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is issued under an offset protocol and offset
    program that is set out on the list published on the Department of
    the Environment's website;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it is eligible for use as a method of compensation
    or compliance with respect to a pricing mechanism for GHG emissions
    in the province in which it was issued; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it corresponds to a GHG reduction or removal of one
    CO~2~e tonne.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offset programs

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In establishing the list of offset programs, the
  Minister must ensure that each one includes the following elements:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} rules regarding governance, oversight and
    enforcement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} rules regarding registration and renewal of
    projects;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} rules regarding the establishment of periods within
    which units or credits may be issued;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} rules regarding who is entitled to the credit or
    unit for the GHG reductions or removals;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} measures to ensure that GHG reductions and removals
    are additional and permanent and that reversal risks have been
    mitigated;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} measures to ensure that, for a GHG reduction or
    removal of one CO~2~e tonne, one credit or unit is issued and the
    unit or credit is not used more than once;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} publicly available information on projects,
    protocols and units or credits; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a requirement that, prior to the issuance of a unit
    or credit, there be a verification by a third party in accordance
    with procedures that allow them to determine with a reasonable level
    of assurance that the associated project meets the requirements of
    the protocol and program.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offset protocols

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In establishing the list of offset protocols
  recognized under a program referred to in subsection (2), the Minister
  must verify that each protocol ensures that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reduction or removal is with respect to a GHG;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reduction or removal is not subject to carbon
    pollution pricing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the GHG reduction or removal is quantified using
    scientifically-established methods that allow for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the calculation of the quantity of GHG emissions
      or removals for each GHG source, sink and reservoir,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the quantification of reversals,

    - [(ii.1)]{.lawlabel} the assessment of leakage risks,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the calculation of the quantity of GHG
      reductions or removals that would not have occurred in comparison
      with the baseline scenario, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the use of conservative quantification
      assumptions and approaches;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} the baseline scenario used reflects the most
    recent data available, any legal requirements and current practices
    with respect to the activity that is the subject of the protocol;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} best practices are used for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} data gathering and management,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} record keeping,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the ongoing monitoring of offset projects,
      including permanence, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} quality assurance and quality control; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the GHG global warming potentials used in
    calculations are less than or equal to those set out in column 2 of
    Schedule 3 to the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recognized unit or credit

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A recognized unit or credit must, at the time of its
  remittance to the Minister,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be valid;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have been issued in respect of a project that uses
    a protocol referred to in subsection (3);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} have been issued in respect of a project located in
    Canada that began in 2017 or later;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} have been verified by a verification body that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is accredited as a verification body to the ISO
      Standard 14065 by the Standards Council of Canada, the ANSI
      National Accreditation Board or any other accreditation
      organization that is a member of the International Accreditation
      Forum, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is not suspended by the accreditation
      organization that issued its accreditation nor suspended at the
      time the verification relative to the issuance of the unit or
      credit occurred or at the time the verification report related to
      the issuance of the unit or credit was signed; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} be designated for use exclusively as a compliance
    unit for the purposes of remittance to the Minister under section
    174, paragraph 178(1)(a) or subsection 181(2) of the Act.

## [Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1184979 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsection 12(3)

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** For the 2019 calendar
year, despite subsection 12(3), if a covered facility where a specified
industrial activity set out in items 2 or 3, column 1, of Schedule 1 is
engaged in produces hydrogen gas, the person responsible for the covered
facility must include in their annual report the information referred to
in that subsection if it is available.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** Section 8 of the
[Greenhouse Gas Emissions Information Production
Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read immediately before
August 1, 2019, continues to apply, with any necessary modifications,
with respect to a person responsible for a covered facility where an
industrial activity set out in Schedule 1 to these Regulations is
engaged in until January 1, 2020.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records

- **[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  records kept in accordance with section 11 of the [Greenhouse Gas
  Emissions Information Production
  Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read immediately before
  August 1, 2019, during the period beginning on January 1, 2019 and
  ending on August 1, 2019, are deemed to be records kept for the
  purposes of subsection 45(1) of these Regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative method

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the 2019 calendar year, if a person responsible
  for a covered facility used an alternative sampling, measurement or
  analysis method for a specified emission type in accordance with
  section 8 of the [Greenhouse Gas Emissions Information Production
  Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read before August 1,
  2019, they must keep a record of a description of that method.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clinker

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** For the 2019 calendar
year, despite paragraph 31(1)(a) of these Regulations, the production
from a covered facility with respect to the specified industrial
activity set out in paragraph 7(a), column 1, of Schedule 1 of these
Regulations may be quantified under paragraph 36(c) of the [Greenhouse
Gas Emissions Information Production
Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read immediately before
August 1, 2019. If the production is quantified under the Order, it
cannot be used in the calculation of the emissions limit under
subsection 36(1) of these Regulations.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Glass containers

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** For the 2019 calendar
year, despite paragraph 31(1)(a) and subsection 36(1) of these
Regulations, the production from a covered facility with respect to the
specified industrial activity set out in paragraph 9(a), column 1, of
Schedule 1 to these Regulations may be quantified in accordance with
section 103.2 of the [Greenhouse Gas Emissions Information Production
Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read before August 1, 2019.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}High value chemicals

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** For the 2019 calendar
year, despite paragraph 31(1)(a) of these Regulations, the production
from a facility with respect to the specified industrial activity set
out in paragraph 17(a), column 1, of Schedule 1 to these Regulations may
be quantified in accordance with section 103.36 of the [Greenhouse Gas
Emissions Information Production Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214),
as it read before August 1, 2019.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Isopropyl alcohol

**[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** For the 2019 calendar
year, despite subparagraph 11(1)(a)(ii) of these Regulations, the person
responsible for the covered facility is not required to quantify the
production from the specified industrial activity set out in paragraph
3(c), column 1, of Schedule 1.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pulp and Paper

- **[[85.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the 2019 calendar year, despite
  paragraph 31(1)(a) and subsection 36(1) of these Regulations, the
  production from a covered facility with respect to the specified
  industrial activity set out in paragraph 36(c), column 1, of Schedule
  1 to these Regulations may be quantified in accordance with section
  102 of the [Greenhouse Gas Emissions Information Production
  Order](/eng/regulations/SOR-2018-214), as it read before August
  1, 2019. In that case, the output-based standards that apply are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 0.203 CO~2~e tonnes per unit of measurement, in the
    case of a facility equipped with a recovery boiler, lime kiln or
    pulping digester, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} 0.184 CO~2~e tonnes per unit of measurement, in the
    case of a facility not equipped with a recovery boiler, lime kiln or
    pulping digester.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reporting

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the 2019 calendar year, if a covered facility
  where the specified industrial activity set out in paragraph 36(c),
  column 1, of Schedule 1 to these Regulations is engaged in quantifies
  production in accordance with subsection (1), the annual report must
  include the quantity of specialty products produced, in tonnes, if
  that information is available.

## [Coming into Force]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1185021 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2019

- **[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (6),
  these Regulations are deemed to have come into force on January 1,
  2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}July 1, 2019

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations apply in Yukon and Nunavut on July
  1, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2020

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Sections 26 to 30 and subsections 31(2) and (3) come
  into force on January 1, 2020.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}August 1, 2019

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 45(2) of these Regulations comes into
  force on August 1, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}January 1, 2022

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 56 comes into force on January 1, 2022.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}February 16, 2021

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Section 76 comes into force on February 16, 2021.

## [SCHEDULE 2]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsection 11(1))]{.OriginatingRef}[Content of Annual Report on Emissions and Production]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1185044 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** Information with respect to the person responsible for the
  covered facility:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an indication as to whether they own or are
    otherwise responsible for the covered facility, including having the
    charge, management or control of the facility, or are the true
    decision maker with respect to the facility's operations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their name (including any trade name or other name
    used by them) and civic address;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number and, if any, email address of their authorized
    official;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses,
    telephone number and, if any, email address of a contact person, if
    different from the authorized official; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the federal Business Number assigned to them by the
    Canada Revenue Agency, if any.

- **2** Information with respect to the covered facility:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} its name and the civic address of its physical
    location, if any;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} its latitude and longitude coordinates in decimal
    degrees or degrees, minutes and seconds, except for a covered
    facility referred to in paragraph (b) of the definition of
    [[facility]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 1(1) of these
    Regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} its six-digit North American Industry
    Classification System (NAICS) Canada code;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the covered facility certificate number that was
    issued to it;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the National Pollutant Release
    Inventory (NPRI) identification number assigned to it for the
    purposes of the [Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
    1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31) and its Greenhouse Gas Reporting Program
    identification number; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an electricity generation facility
    or a covered facility referred to in paragraph 11(1)(c) that is
    composed, in part, of a unit or a group of units,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the unique name for each unit,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the unit's registration number under the
      Regulations Limiting Carbon Dioxide Emissions from Natural
      Gas-fired Generation of Electricity, if any, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the unit's registration number under the
      Reduction of Carbon Dioxide Emissions from Coal-fired Generation
      of Electricity Regulations, if any.

- **3** The quantity of each GHG for the compliance period, expressed in
  tonnes, for each of the following emission types:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} stationary fuel combustion emissions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} industrial process emissions;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} industrial product use emissions;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} venting emissions;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} flaring emissions;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} leakage emissions;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} on-site transportation emissions;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} waste emissions; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} wastewater emissions.

- **3.1** The global warming potential applicable for each GHG for the
  compliance period.

- **4** The quantities of CH~4~ and N~2~O that are subtracted under
  subsection 17(5) or 20(6) of these Regulations from the total CH~4~and
  N~2~O, expressed in tonnes, separately.

- **5** A list of the methods used to calculate, sample, measure and
  analyze each specified emission type and GHG for the compliance
  period.

- **6** If a covered facility uses a continuous emissions monitoring
  system and has captured or stored CO~2~, the quantity of CO~2~
  captured during the compliance period.

- **7** If CO~2~ was captured, stored and deducted from the total
  quantity of GHGs under section 35 of these Regulations, the following
  information,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement that indicates whether the CO~2~ was
    stored in accordance with the requirements set out in subsection
    35(2) of these Regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the type of geological storage site used, among
    those set out in paragraph 35(2)(a) of these Regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the latitude and longitude coordinates in decimal
    degrees or degrees, minutes and seconds, of the storage site; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the owner or the operator
    of the storage site, if they are different than those of the person
    responsible for the covered facility.

- **8** The output-based standard for each of the specified industrial
  activities engaged in at the covered facility.

- **8.1** If an output-based standard must be calculated for a specified
  industrial activity engaged in at the covered facility or recalculated
  under to section 39 of these Regulations, the following information in
  the annual report for the compliance period for which the standard is
  calculated,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the quantification, sampling, analysis and
    measurement methods and the data used to calculate that standard;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a determination is made under subsection 37(2.1)
    of these Regulations, the methods for the projections and the
    projections bearing the stamp and signature of an engineer; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the method used to attribute the quantity of GHGs
    to each specified industrial activity or to activities that are not
    specified industrial activities.

- **9** The method used to determine the covered facility's production
  from each of the specified industrial activities engaged in at the
  facility.

- **10** With respect to thermal energy that is sold or bought, in
  addition to the information required in section 12 of these
  Regulations:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of each covered facility from which
    thermal energy was bought and the covered facility certificate
    number that was issued to it; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of each covered facility to which thermal
    energy was sold and the covered facility certificate number that was
    issued to it.

- **11** With respect to a facility that produces cement,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in addition to the tonnes of grey cement, the
    tonnes of clinker, gypsum and limestone that the grey cement
    contains, separately; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in addition to the tonnes of white cement, the
    tonnes of clinker, gypsum and limestone that the white cement
    contains, separately.

- **12** With respect to a facility that produces petrochemicals, the
  quantity of hydrogen gas produced during the compliance period, in
  tonnes, the quantity of hydrogen gas sold, in tonnes, and the
  concentration of the hydrogen gas sold, expressed in weight
  percentage.

- **13** With respect to a facility that produces metal from the mining
  of ore

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in addition to the total quantity of silver,
    platinum and palladium, in kilograms, combined, each of those metals
    separately, and;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in addition to the combined total of base metals,
    in tonnes combined, each of those base metals in ore concentrate, in
    tonnes, separately.

- **14** With respect to an electricity generation facility composed of
  a unit referred to in subsection 41(2) of these Regulations, a
  statement indicating whether solid fuel was used by the unit to
  generate electricity in 2018 and whether additional liquid or gaseous
  fuel was used in that same year, if that information is available.

- **15** With respect to a covered facility for which part or all of the
  electricity generated from fossil fuels is not quantified, a list of
  the units or equipment from which the electricity is generated but not
  quantified.

- **16** With respect to a covered facility referred to in section 36.2
  or 41.2 of these Regulations, with respect to the units or equipment
  whose electricity generation capacity has been increased during a
  compliance period,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the total capacity and thermal energy to
    electricity ratio of the unit or equipment prior to its capacity
    being increased;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the total capacity and thermal energy to
    electricity ratio of the unit or equipment subsequent to its
    capacity being increased; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the date of completion of any modifications made to
    the unit or equipment to increase its capacity.

- **17** With respect to an electricity generation facility referred to
  in section 41.1 of these Regulations, the total capacity and thermal
  energy to electricity ratio of each unit within the facility that
  generates electricity from gaseous fuels.

::: HistoricalNote

#### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Quantification of Production]{.HTitleText2} {#division-2quantification-of-production .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Direct-only complexity weighted barrels
    (direct-only CWB) is quantified in accordance with the method
    outlined in section 2.5 of the directive entitled CAN-CWB
    Methodology for Regulatory Support: Public Report, published by
    Solomon Associates in January 2014.

  - [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} When quantifying the direct-only complexity
    weighted barrels, the emissions and energy use accounted for are
    those that are associated with the industrial activity set out in
    paragraph 3(a), column 1 of Schedule 1.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the method referred to in subsection (1),

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the value of "Sales and Exports of Steam and
      Electricity" is equal to zero;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the value of "EC Reported CO~2~e Site Emissions"
      excludes

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the emissions associated with electricity
        generated at the covered facility and used for the purpose of
        the industrial activity set out in paragraph 3(a), column 1 of
        Schedule 1, prorated based on the energy used for the refinery
        operation, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the emissions associated with steam generated
        at the covered facility but not used for the purpose of the
        industrial activity set out in paragraph 3(a), column 1 of
        Schedule 1;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the value of "Deemed Indirect CO~2~e Emissions
      from imported electricity"

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} includes emissions associated with electricity
        that is generated and used at the covered facility and used for
        the purpose of the industrial activity set out in paragraph
        3(a), column 1 of Schedule 1, prorated based on the energy used
        for the refinery operation, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for purchased electricity, is calculated using
        0.420 tonnes of CO~2~e per megawatt hours of electricity bought;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the value of "Deemed Indirect CO~2~e Emissions
      from imported steam" is equal to zero; and

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the value of the "CWB factor" used to calculate
      hydrogen generation, in all cases, is 5.7.

- **1** The combined quantity, in cubic metres, of propane and butane
  set out in paragraph 4(b), column 2, of the table to Schedule 1 is the
  sum of the quantity of propane, in cubic metres, at a temperature of
  15°C and at an equilibrium pressure and the quantity of butane at a
  temperature of 15°C and at an equilibrium pressure, in cubic metres.

#### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Quantification of Production]{.HTitleText2} {#division-2quantification-of-production-2 .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Production by the covered facility, expressed
    in megawatt hours, is the sum of the amounts determined by the
    following formula for each of the drivers operated by the covered
    facility:

    ::: {#1438027 .Subsection}
    P~x~ × L~x~× H~x~

    where

    [P~x~]{.dfn}
    :   is the rated brake power of driver "x", expressed in megawatts;

    [L~x~]{.dfn}
    :   is the actual annual average percent load of driver "x", or, if
        the actual annual average percent load is unavailable, the
        percentage determined by the formula:
        ::: {#1438031 .NestedFormula}
        rpm~avg~ /rpm~max~

        where

        [rpm~avg~]{.dfn}
        :   is the actual annual average speed during operation of
            driver "x", expressed in revolutions per minute, and

        [rpm~max~]{.dfn}
        :   is the maximum rated speed of driver "x", expressed in
            revolutions per minute;
        :::

    [H~x~]{.dfn}
    :   is the number of hours during the compliance period that driver
        "x" was operated; and
    :::

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in this section.

    [[driver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[driver]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an electric motor,
        reciprocating engine or turbine used to drive a
        compressor. ([conducteur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    [[rated brake power]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

    :   [[rated brake power]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the maximum
        brake power of a driver as specified by its manufacturer either
        on its nameplate or otherwise. ([puissance au frein
        nominale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  Schedule 1 refers only to clinker that is transported out of the
  facility.

- **2** The quantity of grey cement and white cement set out in
  paragraphs 7(b) and (c), column 2, of Schedule 1 refers only to cement
  produced from clinker that was produced at that facility and that has
  not been transported out of the facility.

#### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Quantification of Production]{.HTitleText2} {#division-2quantification-of-production-5 .SchedHeadL2}

- **1** Production is quantified at the end of the formulation step of
  the manufacturing process, in litres of vaccine, determined by the
  formula:

  :::: {#1438143 .Section}
  ::: AlternateText
  The summation of the products of A and B for each tank "i"
  :::

  <figure>
  <div class="imageGroup">
  <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_023e.png" />
  </div>
  </figure>

  where:

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the capacity of each tank "i" that is used to combine
      ingredients at that step, expressed in litres;

  [B]{.dfn}
  :   is the number of batches produced in tank "i"; and

  [i]{.dfn}
  :   is the i^th^ tank where "i" goes from 1 to n where n is the total
      number of tanks used to combine ingredients for that step.
  ::::

#### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Quantification of Production]{.HTitleText2} {#division-2quantification-of-production-7 .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Production by the covered facility is
    quantified in tonnes of finished product or tonnes of specialty
    product, as follows:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of pulp, including dissolving pulp
      for viscose,

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the moisture content exceeds 10%, the weight
        of the pulp is adjusted so that its moisture content does not
        exceed 10%, and

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the moisture content is equal to or less
        than 10%, the weight of the pulp without adjustment; and

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a finished product or specialty
      product referred to in subsection (3) derived directly from pulp
      or the pulping process, the weight of the product or, if it has
      been machine dried, its weight after it has been dried.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A finished product referred to in paragraph (1)(b)
    does not include pulping liquor, wood waste, non-condensable gases,
    sludge, tall oil, turpentine, biogas, steam, water or products that
    are used in the production process.

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b), a specialty
    product means abrasive paper base, food grade grease resistant
    paper, packaging waxed paper base, paper for medical applications,
    napkin paper for commercial use, towel paper for commercial or
    domestic use, bath paper for domestic use and facial paper for
    domestic use.

#### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Quantification of Production --- Main Industrial Activity]{.HTitleText2} {#division-2quantification-of-production-main-industrial-activity .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 2, if a unit uses only one
    fossil fuel to generate electricity, production of electricity must
    be quantified in Gigawatt hours of gross electricity generated by
    the unit, measured at the electrical terminals of the generators of
    each unit using meters that comply with the requirements set out in
    subsection 31(2) of these Regulations.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 2, if a unit uses a mixture of
    fossil fuels or a mixture of biomass and fossil fuels to generate
    electricity, the gross electricity generated by the unit is to be
    determined separately for the gaseous fuels, liquid fuels and solid
    fuels in accordance with the following formula and expressed in
    Gigawatt hours:

    :::: {#1438328 .Subsection}
    ::: AlternateText
    Gu is multiplied by a quotient where the numerator is HFFk, and the
    denominator is HB plus the summation of HFFk for each gaseous fuels,
    liquid fuels and solid fuels "k".
    :::

    <figure>
    <div class="imageGroup">
    <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_024e.png" />
    </div>
    </figure>

    where

    [G~U~]{.dfn}
    :   is the gross quantity of electricity generated by the unit
        during a compliance period, as measured at the electrical
        terminals of the generators of the unit using meters that comply
        with the requirements set out in subsection 31(2) of these
        Regulations;

    [HFF~k~]{.dfn}
    :   is determined in accordance with the following formula,
        calculated separately for gaseous fuels, liquid fuels and solid
        fuels type "k":
        :::: {#1438333 .NestedFormula}
        ::: AlternateText
        The summation of the products of QFFj and HHVj for each fossil
        fuel type "j".
        :::

        <figure>
        <div class="imageGroup">
        <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_025e.png" />
        </div>
        </figure>

        where

        [QFF~j~]{.dfn}
        :   is the quantity of gaseous, liquid or solid fuel, as the
            case may be, type "j" combusted in the unit to generate
            electricity during the compliance period, determined in
            accordance with subsection (3),

        [HHV~j~]{.dfn}
        :   is the higher heating value of the gaseous, liquid or solid
            fuel, as the case may be, type "j" combusted in the unit,
            determined in accordance with Quantification Methods, and

        [j]{.dfn}
        :   is the j^th^ fossil fuel type combusted in the unit, where
            "j" goes from 1 to m and where m is the number of types of
            gaseous, liquid or solid fuel combusted, as the case may be,
            combusted; and
        ::::

    [HB]{.dfn}
    :   is determined in accordance with the formula
        :::: {#1438340 .NestedFormula}
        ::: AlternateText
        The summation of the products of QBi and HHVi for each biomass
        fuel type "i"
        :::

        <figure>
        <div class="imageGroup">
        <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_026e.png" />
        </div>
        </figure>

        where:

        [QB~i~]{.dfn}
        :   is the quantity of biomass fuel type "i" combusted in the
            unit to generate electricity during the compliance period,
            determined in accordance with the subsection (3),

        [HHV~i~]{.dfn}
        :   is the higher heating value for the biomass fuel type "i"
            combusted in the unit, is determined in accordance with
            Quantification Methods, and

        [i]{.dfn}
        :   is the i^th^ biomass fuel type combusted in the unit, where
            "i" goes from 1 to n and where n is the number of types of
            biomass fuels combusted.
        ::::
    ::::

  - [(3)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of fuel for QFF~j~ or QB~i~ is
    determined on the following basis:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a solid fuel, the mass of the fuel combusted,
      on a wet or dry basis, expressed in tonnes and measured by a
      measuring device;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a liquid fuel, the volume of the fuel
      combusted, expressed in kL and measured using a flow meter; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a gaseous fuel, the volume of the fuel
      combusted, expressed in standard cubic metres and measured using a
      flow meter.

- **2** If a combustion engine unit and a boiler unit share the same
  steam turbine, the quantity of gross electricity generated by a given
  unit is determined by the formula

  ::: {#1438351 .Section}
  G~ce~ + G~s~ -- G~ext~

  where

  [G~ce~]{.dfn}
  :   is the gross quantity of electricity that is generated by the
      generators of the combustion engines in a combustion engine unit
      that shares a steam turbine with a boiler unit, in the calendar
      year, expressed in Gigawatt hours, as measured at the electrical
      terminals of the generators of the combustion engines using meters
      that comply with the requirements set out in subsection 31(2) of
      these Regulations, if the given unit for which the electricity is
      being quantified is a combustion engine unit, or equal to zero, if
      the given unit for which the electricity is being quantified is a
      boiler unit;

  [G~s~]{.dfn}
  :   is the gross quantity of electricity that is generated by the
      generators of the shared steam turbine in the calendar year,
      expressed in Gigawatt hours, as measured at the electrical
      terminals of the generators of the shared steam turbine using
      meters that comply with the requirements set out in subsection
      31(2) of these Regulations; and

  [G~ext~]{.dfn}
  :   is the quantity of electricity that is generated by the unit other
      than the given unit for which the electricity is being quantified,
      in the calendar year, expressed in Gigawatt hours and that is
      determined by the formula
      :::: {#1438356 .NestedFormula}
      ::: AlternateText
      Gext is equal to Gs, multiplied by the summation for all time
      periods "t" of a quotient where the numerator is the summation of
      the products of hext,j and Mext,j for each heat stream "j", and
      the denominator is the summation of the products of hext,j and
      Mext,j for each heat stream "j" plus the summation of the products
      of hint,k and Mint,k for each heat stream "k".
      :::

      <figure>
      <div class="imageGroup">
      <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_027e.png" />
      </div>
      </figure>

      where

      [G~s~]{.dfn}
      :   is the gross quantity of electricity that is generated by the
          generators of the shared steam turbine in the calendar year,
          expressed in Gigawatt hours, as measured at the electrical
          terminals of the generators of the shared steam turbine using
          meters that comply with the requirements set out in subsection
          31(2) of these Regulations,

      [t]{.dfn}
      :   is the t^th^ hour, where "t" goes from the number 1 to x and
          where x is the total number of hours during which the
          generators of the shared steam turbine generated electricity
          in the calendar year,

      [j]{.dfn}
      :   is the j^th^ external heat stream, originating from the other
          unit where "j" goes from the number 1 to m and where m is the
          total number of external heat streams that contributed to the
          electricity generated by the generators of the shared steam
          turbine of the unit,

      [h~ext,j~]{.dfn}
      :   is the average specific enthalpy of the j^th^ external heat
          stream, originating from the other unit that contributed to
          the electricity generated by the generators of the shared
          steam turbine, expressed in gigajoules/tonne, during period
          "t" and must be based on the measurement of the temperature
          and pressure of that heat stream and determined using a
          continuous measuring device,

      [M~ext,j~]{.dfn}
      :   is the mass flow of the j^th^ external heat stream originating
          from the other unit that contributed to the electricity
          generated by the generators of the shared steam turbine,
          expressed in tonnes, during period "t", determined using a
          continuous measuring device,

      [k]{.dfn}
      :   is the k^th^ internal heat stream originating from the given
          unit, where "k" goes from the number 1 to l and where l is the
          total number of heat streams that originated from the
          combustion of fuel in the unit and that contributed to the
          electricity generated by the generators of the shared steam
          turbine,

      [h~int,k~]{.dfn}
      :   is the average specific enthalpy of the k^th^ internal heat
          stream originating from the given unit and having contributed
          to the electricity generated by the generators of the shared
          steam turbine, expressed in GJ/tonne, during period "t" and
          must be based on the measurement of the temperature and
          pressure of that heat stream and determined using a continuous
          measuring device, and

      [M~int,k~]{.dfn}
      :   is the mass flow of the k^th^ internal heat stream originating
          from the given unit that contributed to the electricity
          generated by the generators of the shared steam turbine,
          expressed in tonnes, during period "t", determined using a
          continuous measuring device.
      ::::
  :::

#### [DIVISION 3]{.HLabel2}[Secondary Industrial Activity -- Quantification of Production]{.HTitleText2} {#division-3secondary-industrial-activity-quantification-of-production .SchedHeadL2}

- **3** If a covered facility uses only one fossil fuel to generate
  electricity, production of electricity is quantified in Gigawatt hours
  of gross electricity generated through the use of fossil fuels.

- - **4** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a covered facility uses a mixture of
    fossil fuels or a mixture of biomass and fossil fuels to generate
    electricity, the gross electricity generated by the facility is to
    be determined separately for the gaseous fuels, liquid fuels and
    solid fuels in accordance with the following formula and expressed
    in Gigawatt hours:

    :::: {#1438372 .Subsection}
    ::: AlternateText
    Gu is multiplied by a quotient where the numerator is HFFk, and the
    denominator is HB plus the summation of HFFk for each gaseous fuels,
    liquid fuels and solid fuels "k"
    :::

    <figure>
    <div class="imageGroup">
    <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_028e.png" />
    </div>
    </figure>

    where

    [G~U~]{.dfn}
    :   is the gross quantity of electricity generated by the covered
        facility during the compliance period, expressed in Gigawatt
        hours;

    [HFF~k~]{.dfn}
    :   is determined in accordance with the following formula,
        calculated separately for gaseous fuels, liquid fuels and solid
        fuels type "k":
        :::: {#1438377 .NestedFormula}
        ::: AlternateText
        The summation of the products of QFFj and HHVj for each fossil
        fuel type "j"
        :::

        <figure>
        <div class="imageGroup">
        <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_025e.png" />
        </div>
        </figure>

        where

        [QFF~j~]{.dfn}
        :   is the quantity of gaseous, liquid or solid fuel, as the
            case may be, type "j" combusted in the facility for
            electricity generation during the compliance period,
            determined under subsection (2) and in accordance with
            Quantification Methods,

        [HHV~j~]{.dfn}
        :   is the higher heating value of the gaseous, liquid or solid
            fuel, as the case may be, type "j" combusted in the facility
            for electricity generation determined in accordance with
            Quantification Methods, and

        [j]{.dfn}
        :   is the j^th^ fossil fuel type combusted in the facility,
            where "j" goes from 1 to m and where m is the number of
            types of gaseous, liquid or solid fuels combusted, as the
            case may be; and
        ::::

    [HB]{.dfn}
    :   is determined in accordance with the formula
        :::: {#1438384 .NestedFormula}
        ::: AlternateText
        The summation of the products of QBi and HHVi for each biomass
        fuel type "i"
        :::

        <figure>
        <div class="imageGroup">
        <img src="/images/SOR-2019-266img/SOR-2019-266_029e.png" />
        </div>
        </figure>

        where

        [QB~i~]{.dfn}
        :   is the quantity of biomass fuel type "i" combusted in the
            facility for electricity generation during the compliance
            period, determined in accordance with subsection (2) and
            with Quantification Methods,

        [HHV~i~]{.dfn}
        :   is the higher heating value for each biomass fuel type "i"
            combusted in the facility for electricity generation in
            accordance with Quantification Methods, and

        [i]{.dfn}
        :   is the i^th^ biomass fuel type combusted in the facility,
            where "i" goes from 1 to n and where n is the number of
            types of biomass fuels combusted.
        ::::
    ::::

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of fuel for QFF~j~ and QB~i~ is
    determined on the following basis:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for a solid fuel, the mass of the fuel combusted,
      on a wet or dry basis, expressed in tonnes and measured in
      accordance with Quantification Methods;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for a liquid fuel, the volume of the fuel
      combusted, expressed in kL and measured in accordance with
      Quantification Methods; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for a gaseous fuel, the volume of the fuel
      combusted, expressed in standard cubic metres and measured in
      accordance with Quantification Methods.

### [PART 39]{.HLabel1}[Production of wood products]{.HTitleText1} {#part-39production-of-wood-products .SchedHeadL1}

#### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Quantification of Production]{.HTitleText2} {#division-2quantification-of-production-9 .SchedHeadL2}

- - **1** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of wood veneer does not include
    wood veneer used in the production of plywood.

  - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The quantity of wood veneer and plywood refers only
    to wood veneer and plywood that will not undergo an additional
    transformation at the facility.

- **2** The quantity of lumber produced refers only to lumber that will
  not undergo an additional transformation at the facility.

- **3** The quantity of particle board and of panels composed primarily
  of cellulosic fibers and a bonding system cured under heat and
  pressure, including hardboard, is quantified after they are cured
  under heat and pressure.

- **1** The production is quantified, in tonnes of alumina (Al~2~O~3~)
  equivalent determined by the following formula:

  ::: {#1438441 .Section}
  A x 0.6536

  where

  [A]{.dfn}
  :   is the quantity of alumina hydrate produced at the precipitation
      step, expressed in tonnes.
  :::

::::: section

::: {}
## [SCHEDULE 4]{.scheduleLabel}[(Subsections 27(1) and 29(1))]{.OriginatingRef}[Information to Include in Application for Permit]{.scheduleTitleText}  {#h-1185529 .scheduleLabel}
:::

- **1** Information with respect to the applicant:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name, civic and postal addresses in Canada,
    telephone number and, if any, email address; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic and postal addresses in
    Canada, telephone number and, if any, email address of their
    authorized official.

- **2** The covered facility certificate number that was issued to the
  covered facility for which the application is being submitted.

- **3** Details with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} each prescribed method or guideline that cannot be
    used;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the specified emission type and GHG; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirement that cannot be met.

- **4** Information establishing that, at the time of the application,
  it is not technically or economically feasible for the applicant to
  use the prescribed quantification method or guideline.

- **5** A description of the alternative method to be used for each
  method or guideline in respect of which a permit is sought and
  information that demonstrates that the quantification method being
  proposed is at least as rigorous as the prescribed method or guideline
  and provides equivalent results to those that would have been obtained
  from the prescribed method or guideline.

- **6** The requested term of the permit, which must be the period for
  which the permit is necessary.
::::
:::::
